v0.5.4 Stable2026-11-09T10:08:45Z

中文

主要更新

  • MCP 与实时上下文：注册 Streamable HTTP MCP Server，检索、调用工具，并把工具挂载为可读取的 Markdown 节点。提供 REST、CLI 和 SDK 入口；同步上游工具后可查看受影响的挂载。#6263
  • Agent 规则查询：多种 Agent 集成增加 Rule Search 入口，在执行操作前按需查找适用规则。升级插件或重连 MCP 客户端后生效；这不会自动增加每轮拦截。#7005
  • Watch 多节点运行：增加 MySQL Watch 存储和列表分页；Connector 授权撤销后自动暂停，重新授权时只恢复此前自动暂停的任务。#6756、#6944
  • 会话恢复与记忆整理：失败或失去执行任务的归档支持带内容哈希的显式重试；可用 ov compile memory 整理当前用户的已有记忆。#5802、#6829
  • 配置与访问控制：Account 可独立配置抽取开关、生成语言及解析凭证；普通用户可管理自己的添加位置和记忆策略。新增由 ROOT 管理的 GLOBAL 身份及按数据所有者配置的读取策略。#6986、#7050、#6900、#6932

可选可观测性上报

新增可选 Kafka 事件与存量盘点上报，需要安装 openviking[reporting] 并显式开启，默认关闭。上报保留部署、数据空间、用户和模型费用归属，不包含原文、提示词或 API Key；采用内存队列，允许丢失，不适合作为持久化审计或计费账本。#7143

发布版本

主包 openviking==0.5.4、Python SDK openviking-sdk==0.1.19、独立 CLI 0.5.2。已有 Python SDK 和独立 CLI 用户需要升级相应组件，才能获得本版的组成员接口适配。

pip install --upgrade 'openviking==0.5.4' 'openviking-sdk==0.1.19'

使用示例

以下示例要求已配置对应版本的 ov 和用户凭证。天气 MCP 的地址、工具名及参数需替换为实际服务。

ov mcps add -p viking://agent/tools --wait '{"mcpServers":{"weather":{"url":"https://mcp.example.com/mcp"}}}'
ov mcps find "查询北京当前天气" --limit 5
ov mcps mount viking://agent/tools/weather/current.json viking://resources/live/weather-beijing.md --title "北京实时天气" --args '{"city":"Beijing"}' --wait
ov read viking://resources/live/weather-beijing.md

ov compile memory --instruction "整理已有记忆，保留不同对象的独立事实"

读取实时节点只会透明调用符合只读要求的工具。显式声明为非只读或破坏性的工具，需要通过 ov mcps invoke 调用。

升级与迁移

  1. 共享存储部署不能新旧混跑：锁租约协议发生变化。先停止所有锁参与者（包括队列 worker），再统一部署新版本。SQLite 自动增加 holders 列；不要丢弃已有队列 handoff。
  2. 组成员移除 HTTP 接口变更：从 DELETE .../members/{user_id} 改为 DELETE .../members，请求体为 {"user_ids":["alice","bob"]}。本版 CLI、Python/Go SDK 已适配批量端点，原有单用户方法仍可调用；直接使用旧 HTTP 路径的调用方需要迁移。
  3. 单文件恢复参数变更：POST /api/v1/snapshot/restore 的单文件模式改用 restore_commit_id、current_commit_id，两者均为完整 40 位 commit ID；不能继续传原有版本 UUID。目录恢复仍使用原有入口。
  4. Python/Go SDK 移除旧配置方法：Agent Evolution 和旧 Account settings 方法已删除，调用方应迁移至 Account configuration HTTP 接口。服务端旧 HTTP 适配器本版本仍保留并标记弃用。
  5. CacheFS 配置收敛：删除 max_file_size_bytes、traversal_mode、bypass_prefixes。缓存改为文件系统查询结果缓存。Redis 默认连接超时改为 10 秒、命令超时为 0（不设超时）、缓存 TTL 为 300 秒；依赖原默认值的部署应显式配置。
  6. Noul rerank 默认变化：默认隔离候选；需要旧的共享候选行为时显式设置 candidates_visible=true。Choice 模式不受此开关影响。

MySQL Watch 是可选能力：启用前安装 MySQL 依赖、执行建表脚本，并为所有节点配置相同的凭证加密密钥。默认 file Watch 仍只适用于单进程。

修复

  • 恢复跨目录并发：创建新的 Watch、目录和目录迁移时只锁定实际目标范围，不再因兄弟目录正在处理而扩大到公共父目录；删除多级不存在路径时不额外创建空目录。#7265

  • 文件写入与目录刷新分离：写入仍等待文件自身摘要和向量完成，直接父目录的 L0/L1 与目录向量独立刷新；慢目录处理不再延长正文锁等待。#7268

  • 保持组成员客户端方法可用：CLI、Python 和 Go SDK 的单成员添加/移除方法使用批量端点和单元素请求体。#7258

  • 修复训练、记忆替换后的 case/experience 链接维护，以及 LangChain Store 生命周期、持久化和 Unicode 归档查询。#6898、#7038、#7039、#7040

  • 保留旧添加位置配置兼容：viking://user/resources、viking://user/skills 在加载时转换为当前用户家目录 URI，避免沿用旧 ov.conf 升级后无法启动。#7140

  • Go SDK 的 AddSkill、UpdateSkill 可接收较长的内联 Markdown，不再因本地文件名长度限制而提前失败。#7103

  • 飞书表格单元格、嵌入白板，以及带空 ATX 标题或 CRLF frontmatter 的 Markdown 内容解析。#7083、#7075、#7117、#6819

  • 关闭去重的 recall 不再创建会话；eager prefetch 尊重大于 5 的 prefetch_search_topn，并正确序列化时间元数据。#7064、#6925、#6977

  • 改善记忆抽取修复截断时的有效操作保留、记忆拆分后的链接维护，以及会话压缩的历史大小控制。#7052、#5922、#6771

  • 改善上游流式错误传递、断连后的工具流清理、任务取消及跨节点锁续租行为。#6995、#6892、#7082、#6718

首次贡献者

感谢本版本的 11 位首次贡献者：名单见下方中英文共用的贡献者列表。

English

Highlights

  • MCP and live context: Register Streamable HTTP MCP servers, discover and invoke tools, and mount tools as readable Markdown nodes through REST, CLI and SDK interfaces. Sync reports affected mounts. #6263
  • Rule discovery across agent integrations: Query applicable rules before taking an action. Update the plugin or reconnect the MCP client to discover the new tool; this does not add an automatic per-turn enforcement hook. #7005
  • Distributed Watches: Optional MySQL storage, paginated listing, and connector credential lifecycle handling. Reauthorization resumes only Watches paused automatically by credential revocation. #6756, #6944
  • Archive recovery and memory consolidation: Explicit, hash-bound retry for failed or ownerless archives; use ov compile memory to consolidate existing memories for the current user. #5802, #6829
  • Scoped configuration and access control: Account-level extraction, language and parser credentials, user-managed settings, and ROOT-managed GLOBAL identities with data-owner access policies. #6986, #7050, #6900, #6932

The CLI examples above show registration, discovery, mounting and reading of a live node, followed by memory consolidation. Replace the example MCP server and tool schema with your actual service. Only tools meeting the read-only requirements execute transparently during reads; invoke non-read-only or destructive tools explicitly.

Published versions

Main package openviking==0.5.4, Python SDK openviking-sdk==0.1.19, and standalone CLI 0.5.2. Upgrade the corresponding client package or binary to receive the group-membership adaptation.

Optional observability reporting

Optional Kafka events and inventory export require openviking[reporting] and explicit enablement. Reporting preserves deployment, workspace, user and model-cost ownership without source text, prompts or API keys. Its in-memory queue allows loss and is not a durable audit or billing ledger. #7143

Upgrade and migration

  1. Do not mix old and new lock participants on shared storage. Stop all participants, including queue workers, before deploying the new lease protocol. SQLite adds the holders column automatically; retain persisted queue handoffs.
  2. Group member removal uses a batch body. Replace DELETE .../members/{user_id} with DELETE .../members and {"user_ids":["alice","bob"]}. The CLI and Python/Go SDK now adapt existing single-user methods to the collection route; direct callers of the old HTTP path must migrate.
  3. Single-file restore uses commit IDs. Supply restore_commit_id and current_commit_id, both full 40-character commit IDs, instead of version UUIDs. The directory restore interface is unchanged.
  4. Legacy configuration methods are removed from the Python and Go SDKs. Migrate Agent Evolution and old Account settings calls to the Account configuration HTTP API. Deprecated server-side HTTP adapters remain in this release.
  5. CacheFS now caches query results. Remove max_file_size_bytes, traversal_mode and bypass_prefixes. Redis defaults become a 10-second connection timeout, no command timeout, and a 300-second cache TTL; configure explicit values when needed.
  6. Noul reranking isolates candidates by default. Set candidates_visible=true to retain shared candidate visibility. Choice mode is unaffected by this option.

MySQL Watch storage requires the MySQL dependency, schema initialization and the same credential encryption key on every participating node. File-backed Watches remain single-process.

Fixes

  • Restore sibling-path concurrency for Watch creation, directory creation and transfers by locking the actual target scope. Idempotent removal of deeply missing paths does not create empty parents. #7265

  • Separate file processing from direct-parent L0/L1 and directory-vector refresh. File writes still wait for their own summaries and vectors; slow directory refresh no longer extends the content-lock wait. #7268

  • Keep existing single-member CLI, Python and Go methods working through collection membership routes and singleton request bodies. #7258

  • Preserve case/experience links during training and memory replacement; correct LangChain Store ownership, persistence and Unicode archive queries. #6898, #7038, #7039, #7040

  • Preserve legacy add-target configuration by translating viking://user/resources and viking://user/skills to current-user home URIs during configuration loading, preventing startup failures with existing ov.conf files. #7140

  • Accept long inline Markdown in Go SDK AddSkill and UpdateSkill without failing on local filename-length limits. #7103 This release improves Feishu table and whiteboard parsing, Markdown parsing, sessionless recall, eager-prefetch limits and datetime serialization, memory-operation recovery and link maintenance, session compression, upstream stream errors, cancellation and distributed lock renewal. See the linked PRs in the Chinese section for the exact changes.

@upstash/context7-mcp@4.6.0 Stable2026-11-09T13:13:22Z

Minor Changes

  • 2111b92: Add the query-code MCP tool and the ctx7 code command. They search and read a library's source code at the version its docs describe: grep with ripgrep's options, plus definition, references, outline, read and list. ctx7 library marks the libraries whose source is searchable, and resolve-library-id does so with the research preview on. The MCP tool is part of the research preview: it is listed only with --research-preview, CONTEXT7_RESEARCH_PREVIEW=1 or ?research_preview=1.
  • 7765f35: Add the get-upgrade-guide research preview tool: everything that changes between two versions of a library in one response, from the release notes and guides of every release in between and the public API diff of both versions.

trycua/cua ↗ 6 releases · 4 stable · 2 prerelease
cua-driver-rs-v0.45.2cua-driver-rs: v0.45.2 Prerelease2026-11-09T12:26:20Z

Cua Driver 0.45.2

Summary

This release includes 3 fixes.

Fixes

  • wait for a foreground-activated macOS control to report enabled. (#7041)
  • spawn owned-discovery fixture with only its named listeners. (#7048)
  • report the KWin capture switch and desktop entry as KWin reads them. (#7042) Thanks @astrovm.

Contributors

Thanks to @astrovm for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.45.2-nightly.20261109.42561464362 Prerelease2026-11-09T05:25:52Z

Cua Driver 0.45.2-nightly.20261109.42561464362 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Source

  • Commit: c62abf39ba407f385701b0a82a5a4d8b8eacc426
  • Changes: nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.43.3-nightly.20261108.42446386787…c62abf39ba40

Changes

  • Test: cover trusted_session_call on the socket while permissions are pending. (#6934)
  • Feat: capture the KWin desktop through ScreenShot2 behind kwin-capture. (#6890) Thanks @astrovm, @shellwen.
  • Feat: write the KWin desktop entry for the install that owns the CLI. (#6836) Thanks @astrovm.
  • Fix: type exact text on the active keyboard layout or refuse it. (#6827) Thanks @astrovm.
  • Fix: name the dialog that blocks keys and how to reach a spreadsheet cell (CUA-3763). (#6937)
  • Fix: clear portal-input clippy lints in platform-linux. (#6940)
  • Fix: renumber Linux list_windows z_index after its filters. (#6938)
  • Fix: call Text Input Sources only with the thread affinity macOS requires (CUA-3763). (#6939)
  • Fix: cover foreground activation enabling a macOS AppKit control. (#6947)

stablyai/orca ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v1.4.2551.4.255 Stable2026-11-09T12:24:14Z

Faster session search, agents on your phone & smoother chat switching ✨

We shipped two releases in quick succession: 1.4.254 yesterday and 1.4.255 today. See the 1.4.254 release notes for Git Graph, chat forks, on-device phone dictation, more resilient mobile work, and pairing and trust improvements.

A little less waiting, a little more flow. This update makes large session histories easier to search, brings your agents together on your phone, and smooths out the everyday work of switching accounts, models and workspaces.

  • ⚡ Find past work without slowing down: Session History searches run off the app’s main thread, keep results visible while you type, and show your last list immediately after a restart while it refreshes. Search also works on SSH and remote-server hosts.
  • 📱 Your agents, together on your phone: A new Agents tab lists agents across your connected desktops. Mark them read or clear them from the list, with activity status supplied by the desktop. Saved task lists and host labels appear sooner when you reopen the app.
  • 💬 Keep your chat moving: Switching Codex accounts moves your open Codex chats to the account you choose. Forks preserve the chat’s history, pasted rich text keeps its links, and queued messages resume after a restart.
  • 🎛️ A clearer way to choose your setup: Agent speed is now one button, model setup opens with your current choice highlighted, and searching for “codex” puts the Codex agent ahead of other agents’ Codex-named models—including when you press Enter.
  • 🌳 Do more from where you are: Check out, cherry-pick or revert commits from Git Graph. Open the branch picker directly from the branch name under your chat composer, and choose your next action from a simpler empty split pane.

Highlights

  • Session History & performance: Large histories stay responsive while search runs in a worker. Workspace and Project filters query their own sessions, unchanged history rows avoid extra rendering, and search indexing and profile saves do less disk work.
  • Chat & agents: Open Codex chats follow account switches, newly installed skills appear in Codex’s $ menu before your first message, and Antigravity Chat shows installed skills. OpenCode 2 starts with the model you picked. Clearing a chat hides the old transcript immediately, and a failed model or effort change affects its message rather than the whole chat.
  • Phone & remote hosts: The Agents tab brings activity across desktops into one place. Pages open sooner through prewarming, Review refreshes branch comparisons when Git changes, and the file-preview restore bar stays above the keyboard. A stalled LAN reconnect can fall back to Relay, and resumed VMs keep their device pairing when the host key is unchanged.
  • Workspaces & source control: Git Graph gains checkout, cherry-pick and revert actions. You can preserve eligible child workspaces when deleting a parent, create a worktree for each repository from an empty folder workspace, and clone into a separate folder when a repository name is already taken. The sidebar divider resizes the sidebar reliably.
  • Browser, editor & terminal: Remote browser tabs show site favicons, website links open in new tabs, and the CLI can list and reload installed browser extensions. PDFs fit the preview pane’s width. Terminal panes explain when another window is holding their size and offer a way to undo it.
  • Everyday polish: Long Settings lists use searchable pickers, older chat messages show dates, and live agents stay visible in the unread filter. This release also fixes stale browser, Linear toolbar and usage-tooltip displays, plus the first Enter keypress after turning off Japanese IME.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/stablyai/orca/compare/v1.4.254...v1.4.255

v1.4.2541.4.254 Stable2026-11-08T23:25:31Z

Git Graph, on-phone dictation & side questions in chat ✨

  • 🌳 See the whole repository: A new Git Graph tab shows local and remote branches and tags, with each commit's details and changed files. Folder workspaces now get Source Control, with a card for each attached repo, and the branch name in Source Control switches or creates a branch in place.
  • 💬 Ask without derailing a chat: In Chat UI, /btw asks a side question in a fork beside the chat while the original keeps running, and /fork branches from the latest response. Chats that open or push to a pull request show a short line when its CI passes or fails.
  • 🎙️ Dictate from anywhere: Phones can turn speech into text on the device (iPhone on iOS 26 or later, and Android), with the computer as fallback. The computer, phones and web client can dictate at the same time, and on-device dictation runs in its own process, so a speech engine crash or hang no longer takes Orca down.
  • 📱 A phone that holds on to your work: Chat sends queue while the desktop is unreachable and go out in order when it's back. Chat drafts, attachments, unsaved file edits and automation drafts survive the app being killed, and screens reopen from what the phone already has.
  • ⌨️ No more typing lag from Codex accounts: Typing could lag across Orca while it linked Codex history between accounts. That work no longer blocks the app. Remote terminals also send a lone keystroke right away and read compressed output from remote hosts.
  • 🔐 Safer pairing and trust: A pairing code stops working once the device that used it saves its own sign-in, desktop and CLI connections to remote servers use the newer encryption, and orca serve refuses connections from public internet addresses unless there's a reason to allow them. Orca asks once per new repo before trusting it for agents.
  • 🎨 Make Orca yours: Import your own app icon, let theme plugins play a looping background video and ship their own fonts, and read Settings in titled cards. Claude Code can match your Orca terminal theme if you say yes.
  • 🤖 Better agent support: Antigravity chats can run on the host's own agy, including Full access chats, and Muse shows its subscription quota in the status bar.

Highlights

  • Chat: /btw asks a side question in a fork and /fork forks from the latest response, with a spinner while the fork opens. Chats show CI results for the pull requests they opened or pushed to, and every waiting message, command and steer now sits in one ordered queue you can move, delete or send from.
  • Phone: Send while the desktop is offline, keep drafts, attachments, file edits and automation drafts across app kills, and see the workspace list, session tabs and Review from memory while they refresh. Large folders load 500 entries at a time with Search all, Review prefetches the next and previous file, and a thin line under the header shows when cached data is refreshing.
  • Dictation: Phones can transcribe on the device, and every device can dictate at once. On desktop the speech model loads before your first dictation (a new setting chooses when to unload it), dictation stops after a minute of silence and keeps what you said, dictated text gets a space before it, and text that can't be inserted is copied instead. The paired web client can dictate through the host.
  • Source control & editor: Git Graph, Source Control for folder workspaces, and an in-place branch picker in the Source Control header. Wholly new and deleted files show one clean tint, Markdown keeps its syntax colors under plugin themes, HTML previews open in the editor's own tab group, and Copy as PNG says when an image is too large instead of claiming it copied.
  • Agents & accounts: The Codex account-history typing lag is fixed. Antigravity chats can run on the host's agy when it's signed in, Antigravity sessions in AI Vault can reveal their artifacts folder, and Muse shows its quota and keeps panes working while background subagents run. The new-tab launch menu is simpler, the agent picker finds models from its submenus, and after a restart agents are told to finish the original task.
  • Remote hosts: Remote terminals respond to keystrokes sooner, desktop chats on remote hosts stream only the new text, and polling slows down while the Orca window is visible but not focused. Managed SSH servers recover after a slow ProxyCommand login and in more dead-server cases, and automations for a managed SSH host go to its own Orca server.
  • Look & feel: Import a custom app icon, use themed menus and clearer segmented controls, and use web fonts and looping background videos from plugins. Theme plugins can also style chat text and selection, which is now easier to read in dark mode. File and browser tabs fit their titles, so more fit before the tab strip scrolls.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Claude Code theme matching: Orca can now match Claude Code's theme to your Orca terminal theme. It is off for everyone, including new profiles. The next time Claude Code runs, Orca asks once in a toast. Nothing in ~/.claude/settings.json changes unless you accept, and you can change it later in Settings › Appearance › Terminal.
  • Search shortcut: New Mac profiles open search with Cmd+K and have no default shortcut for clearing the terminal. New Windows and Linux profiles use Ctrl+K. Existing profiles keep their shortcuts, including custom and disabled ones.
  • Agent folder trust: The first time you start an agent in a new repo, Orca asks whether to trust the repo or let each agent ask for itself. Repos you already use keep working as before, with no prompt. You can change the answer in the repo's settings.
  • Single-use pairing codes: When an updated phone, browser or Orca pairs with an updated desktop, it switches to its own sign-in and the QR code or link it used stops working. Devices on older apps keep working, and Settings marks devices that still use the original pairing code or older encryption so you know which to update.
  • orca serve and public addresses: orca serve accepts localhost, LAN and Tailscale connections by default and accepts public internet addresses only when there's a reason to. Existing setups keep connecting.
  • Speech model memory: With voice on, Orca now loads the on-device speech model ahead of your first dictation. Use the new Unload Speech Model setting in Settings › Voice to free that memory sooner. Desktop dictation now also stops by itself after a minute of silence.
  • Windows terminal font weight: Terminal text on Windows now uses the font's Regular weight by default instead of Medium, which Windows drew as SemiBold. macOS and Linux are unchanged.
  • New-tab launch menu: Permissions and Arguments are no longer in the new-tab agent launch menu. They still come from Agent settings.
  • Onboarding: The Antigravity Install button in onboarding is gone. Antigravity has the same Install instructions link as every other agent.

rohitg00/agentmemory ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.33 Stable2026-11-09T12:21:23Z

Drop-in upgrade on iii 0.25.1. Docker installs that set only an LLM key: add AGENTMEMORY_LLM_PROVIDER (see Changed).

Added

  • Stores above 64 MiB keep working: whole-scope reads page, transport cap raised to 64 MiB (#1636, #1634)
  • Session list filters startedAfter, startedBefore, endedAfter, endedBefore (#1527)
  • AGENTMEMORY_SUMMARY_TIMEOUT_MS for large sessions on slow or local LLMs (#1689)
  • memory_diagnose / memory_heal find and remove orphaned facets (#1698)

Fixed

  • Search no longer caps saved memories at three per query (#1696); evicted memories leave the indexes (#1601)
  • Boot detects gaps in a partially embedded vector index (#1687); CLIP text-to-image search uses the text tower (#1250)
  • PreToolUse context reaches the model (#1693); enrichment scoped to the current project (#1220); SessionEnd has time to finish (#1692)
  • Redaction runs before truncation and covers compound secret key names (#1695, #1701)
  • Placeholder keys like sk-... count as unset (#1588); circuit breaker resets on success and ignores content-filter rejections (#1350, #1277)
  • MCP shim waits 190 s, so long consolidate and reflect calls finish (#1691)
  • Consolidation keeps provenance across retries (#1545, #1690); scoped reflection stays in its project (#1544)
  • connect honors CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR (#1703); Cursor subagent stop fields mapped (#1684)
  • Fresh installs retry the iii engine download on timeouts, 408, 429 and 5xx (#1719)
  • health CPU is machine-relative (#1286); demo stops its workers on Windows (#1688); Compose values keep newlines (#1708)

Changed

  • Pins iii 0.25.1 with state 0.22.22 and http 0.22.8; first start installs them (#1634, #1694)
  • Unpaged GET /memories and /relations over the transport limit return 413 with a paging hint; use ?limit=N (#1636)
  • Root and Coolify Compose files forward AGENTMEMORY_LLM_PROVIDER (#1700)

Tested

Packed release gate on Linux x64 and arm64, macOS arm64 and Intel, Windows x64 and arm64 (#1716).

Contributors

Thanks @yxtay, @DimitriChrysafis, @mvanhorn, @anhtahaylove, @devon3000, @mothieras, @dmazhukov, @redeye1011, @dKaulig, @DanielCarmingham, @Mike-Animal-Counseling and @zhichao-poper.

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v0.22.4 Stable2026-11-09T12:14:12Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.4

Summary

This patch release ships three improvements to the resume command: exit-time resume hints for better workflow continuation, fixes for directory overrides in resume commands, and corrections to quote escaping.

Added

  • PRINT RESUME BLOCK ON EXIT FOR WORKFLOW CONTINUATION (#4061, #4060): The CLI now prints a resume-hint block when exiting after a work session, enabling users to resume their previous workflow context. The omx resume --last command errors appropriately when no previous session exists, providing clear guidance instead of silently succeeding with empty context.

Fixed

  • HONOR DIRECTORY OVERRIDES IN RESUME COMMANDS (#4062): The resume hint now respects the -C/--cd directory override flags and worktree-specific Codex home paths, ensuring session resolution uses the correct context when launched with directory overrides.
  • RESTORE APOSTROPHE ESCAPING IN SHELLQUOTE (#4063): Fixed quote-escaping regression where apostrophes in shell-quoted values were incorrectly escaped, causing shell syntax errors in paths and identifiers containing apostrophes.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.4.

Verification

  • Full CI passed on dev c81c0aa4 (the source of this release), CI run 42603444740.
  • PR #4061 CI passed on its merged head.
  • PR #4062 CI passed on its merged head.
  • PR #4063 CI passed on its merged head.

Contributors

Thanks to @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

v0.22.3 Stable2026-11-08T21:14:57Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.3

Summary

This patch release ships Plan and Ralplan skill guidance that matches the tools the host actually exposes.

Fixed

  • ALIGN PLAN AND RALPLAN WITH AVAILABLE HOST TOOLS (#3998): The Plan and Ralplan skills no longer reference unavailable ask_codex syntax or a nonexistent $plan consensus mode. Plan asks questions through omx question in attached tmux (with the return pane preserved) and through native structured input elsewhere, uses only the host's exposed reviewer subagents, and reports a missing independent review instead of claiming approval. A "skip planning" request now continues through direct execution while keeping active workflow gates, instead of always handing off to $ultragoal. Ralplan owns the consensus sequence and its quality gates.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.3.

Verification

  • Full CI passed on dev f7569d2b8 (the source of this release), CI run 42516047121.
  • PR #3998 CI passed on its merged head.

Contributors

Thanks to @ev78394 (#3998) and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

v0.22.2 Stable2026-11-08T17:57:57Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.2

Summary

This patch release ships a team-runtime safety fix: integration and cleanup now preserve manual Git operations, corrupted task state, and completion evidence instead of aborting, dropping, or deleting them.

Fixed

  • PRESERVE WORK AND STATE ACROSS TEAM INTEGRATION AND CLEANUP (#4053): Team integration and shutdown no longer abort a manual Git operation (rebase, merge, cherry-pick, revert, bisect) in the leader or a worker worktree; they stop and report it, and only abort a merge or cherry-pick they started themselves. A malformed, id-mismatched or unreadable task file now fails with team_task_read_failed instead of silently vanishing from completion and cleanup gates. Valid ignored completion-evidence reports are archived under the git common dir (omx-completion-evidence/) before worktree removal, so cleanup no longer refuses or deletes them; unknown files still block removal.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Teams with a corrupted task file now get an explicit team_task_read_failed error instead of a silently shorter task list. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.2.

Verification

  • Full CI passed on dev 09fc2b47 (the source of this release), CI run 42501034833.
  • PR #4053 CI passed 26/26 on its merged head 240492a3, including the 30 new p1-safety team tests.

Contributors

Thanks to @shenyankm (#4053) and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

debpalash/VoiceStudio ↗ 2 releases · 2 prerelease
v0.5.8-beta.3v0.5.8-beta.3 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-09T11:41:42Z

AMD and Intel GPUs now speed up OmniVoice, and setup gets far more forgiving. OmniVoice GGUF runs on AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, dots.tts arrives on Windows with no Python setup, and a failing PyTorch download or a broken GPU no longer leaves you without a working app.

New since beta.2: updates now download in the background and install when you quit, with a title-bar pill for progress, Restart and Retry; Clone and Design render at best quality by default and offer faster settings only when a take is measured to be slow; you can run VoiceStudio on a Python you already have; model licence warnings clear once you accept the terms; and a more compact top bar.

Download

Platform Installer
Windows x64 Installer
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer
macOS Apple Silicon DMG
macOS Intel DMG
Linux x64 AppImage · deb
Android APK · AAB
iPhone Unsigned IPA (sideload with your own Apple ID)

Upgrading: Install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept.

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux
  • dots.tts voice cloning now runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery, changing only the voice
  • Dubbing exports subtitles before dubbing and moves lines between speakers
  • Setup falls back to the CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails

Changed

  • Updates now download in the background and install when you quit, with a compact title-bar pill for progress, Restart and Retry, clearer error messages and a restart prompt when work is running (#3928)
  • The top bar is more compact: the account button is just your profile photo, or a person icon when signed out, and the GitHub star count is shortened (#3922).
  • Product analytics moved from PostHog to Supermetry, still only after a yes at the first-run prompt, and now also records which engine ran each generation and transcription. (#3902)
  • Restyled Settings → VoiceStudio account to match the home page: one account card with your plan and storage, the email shown once, and a storage bar visible at 0%. (#3885)
  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)
  • Mac releases use an Apple API key for notarization instead of an account password. (#3775) — thanks @velixio!
  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout. (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)
  • The OmniVoice sidecar availability check no longer imports the model, so backend startup reconcile finishes about 6 s sooner (#3534) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Added

  • Clone and Design now render at each engine's best quality by default, and offer faster settings with a time estimate only when a take is measured to be slow on your computer (#3929)
  • Add GUI selection and bounded discovery of existing Python environments with explicit confirmation and consent-gated dependency repair. (#3701) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • While signed in with account features on, the app sends a periodic check-in with its name, version and operating system; it stops on sign-out or when account features are off. (#3892)
  • The first-run setup screen shows the detected GPU and whether the install uses Metal, CUDA, ROCm or the CPU build. (#3891)
  • The first-run setup screen shows the app version and channel, offers a pending update before the runtime download, and keeps install help visible. (#3890)
  • An available desktop update can be downloaded and installed from a notice on any screen, including first-run setup, and update errors read plainly instead of showing raw server responses (#3881)
  • Development cleanup removes project dependencies, environments, build outputs and default app data, with a dry-run preview (#3877)
  • Dub, batch dub and Tools → Translate offer Kabyle (Taqbaylit) as a translation target (#3740) — thanks @Aksel103!
  • Add per-engine local model folders with authorized discovery, compatibility checks and non-destructive default reset. (#3702) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • Clone takes can be saved as MP3 as well as WAV from the save button's format menu (#3694) — thanks @lucassmonn!
  • Audiobook and Stories scripts take a per-line [emotion:...] direction (one line scared, the next excited) on IndexTTS2, CosyVoice 3 and VoxCPM2; other engines and the phone apps skip the tag instead of reading it aloud (#3664) — thanks @sidmax7!
  • Settings → Account has a Refresh button that reloads your profile and plan (#3850)
  • When the large PyTorch download keeps failing, setup now offers a much smaller CPU version so you still get a working app (#3846)
  • VoiceStudio now tests your GPU on startup and uses the CPU, with a notice, if the GPU fails (#3845)
  • Crash reports now show what was running and how much memory was free, so out-of-memory crashes are easy to spot (#3844)
  • Install and startup failures now name a cause, show the fix first and fill in their own bug report; setup warns about Smart App Control and tests Python before the large download (#3810)
  • Add an Arch Linux development source package with package-managed updates (#3771)
  • Add Android release updates through Obtainium and a publisher-operated F-Droid repository. (#3765)
  • Prepare a bundled, signed runtime and persistent folder grants for private Mac App Store validation. (#3755)
  • Allow explicit external TestFlight beta uploads while keeping internal testing as the default. (#3749)
  • Let Android and iOS users try models below the recommended RAM, with clear warnings and the existing licence and download checks. (#3738)
  • Add native Donate, Pro information and Other Apps pages to iOS and Android, with offline artwork and translated copy. (#3728)
  • Opt-in Settings switch fills [pause] markers and audiobook gaps with the take's own room tone instead of digital silence (#3615, #3611) — thanks @carmoe-prog for the idea!

Fixed

  • Clear stale synthesis and model-selection warnings after all required model licences are accepted (#3910)
  • A backend that exits without a traceback now explains itself: the report carries its last 20 stderr lines, hard exits name their caller, and idle model release can no longer end the process (#3904)
  • Desktop updates keep working when GitHub release downloads fail, by retrying through the GitHub API; a failed update check retries within an hour instead of six, and a stalled download is cancelled and retried (#3899)
  • Updated PyTorch to 2.10 to close a remote-code-execution flaw in checkpoint loading (CVE-2026-24747); AMD Linux GPU installs now use ROCm 7.1. (#3884)
  • Settings → Updates shows the current channel clearly, retries when GitHub briefly fails to respond, and no longer leaves Releases or Database backup loading forever (#3881)

v0.5.8-beta.2v0.5.8-beta.2 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-08T21:13:01Z

Installer signing

Platform Signing
macOS (Apple Silicon) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS (Intel) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Windows x64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Linux x64 — Linux packages are not code-signed

Phone downloads

Android: signed APK · Play bundle.

iPhone: unsigned sideload IPA. Sign with your own Apple ID to install; this is not a TestFlight build.

New since beta.1: an update prompt on every screen, including setup, that also works when GitHub downloads are down; setup shows the app version, your GPU and the build it will install, with help links always visible; a redesigned account page; opt-in analytics moved to Supermetry; clearer reports when the backend stops unexpectedly; five community fixes for dubbing, project bundles, IndexTTS seeds and health reporting; and PyTorch 2.10, which closes a checkpoint-loading security flaw (CVE-2026-24747).

AMD and Intel GPUs now speed up OmniVoice, and setup gets far more forgiving. OmniVoice GGUF runs on AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, dots.tts arrives on Windows with no Python setup, and a failing PyTorch download or a broken GPU no longer leaves you without a working app.

Download

Platform Installer
Windows x64 Installer
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer
macOS Apple Silicon DMG
macOS Intel DMG
Linux x64 AppImage · deb
Android APK · AAB
iPhone Unsigned IPA (sideload with your own Apple ID)

Upgrading: Install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept.

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux
  • dots.tts voice cloning now runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery, changing only the voice
  • Dubbing exports subtitles before dubbing and moves lines between speakers
  • Setup falls back to the CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails

Changed

  • Product analytics moved from PostHog to Supermetry, still only after a yes at the first-run prompt, and now also records which engine ran each generation and transcription. (#3902)
  • Restyled Settings → VoiceStudio account to match the home page: one account card with your plan and storage, the email shown once, and a storage bar visible at 0%. (#3885)
  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)

  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout. (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)

  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Fixed

  • A backend that exits without a traceback now explains itself: the report carries its last 20 stderr lines, hard exits name their caller, and idle model release can no longer end the process (#3904)
  • Desktop updates keep working when GitHub release downloads fail, by retrying through the GitHub API; a failed update check retries within an hour instead of six, and a stalled download is cancelled and retried (#3899)
  • Updated PyTorch to 2.10 to close a remote-code-execution flaw in checkpoint loading (CVE-2026-24747); AMD Linux GPU installs now use ROCm 7.1. (#3884)
  • Settings → Updates shows the current channel clearly, retries when GitHub briefly fails to respond, and no longer leaves Releases or Database backup loading forever (#3881)
  • Importing a shared project bundle no longer fails with a bogus "invalid project archive" on Windows when its title needs characters outside the system code page (#3871) — thanks @RerankerGuo!
  • IndexTTS honors requested generation seeds inside its inference worker (#3870) — thanks @swax!
  • Dub transcription honors supported source-language hints and keeps automatic detection for languages its ASR cannot accept (#3869) — thanks @swax!
  • Large dubbing uploads from the desktop app stream directly to the local backend instead of buffering the entire file first (#3868) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Linux builds no longer drop the Vulkan GPU module at random when the header check races the compiler (#3866)

  • Desktop updates download only the changed parts of the installer on Windows, macOS and the Linux AppImage instead of the whole app, and the offered size no longer counts every installer in the release (#3861)

  • Prevent startup crashes from incorrectly encoded environment files. (#3621)
  • CosyVoice and the desktop bridge pick up hydra-core 1.3.8 and rustls 0.23.46; installed CosyVoice asks for one repair and keeps its model (#3615, #3597, #3598)
  • Android phones download models through hf-mirror.com CDN subdomains like iPhones do, and both refuse redirects to non-standard ports (#3615, #3609) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • The backend no longer fails to start on Windows when a system tool prints characters the locale can't decode (#3615, #3612) — thanks @hachmit87-lab for the report!
  • The Galician interface now includes the expressive speech strings, which fixes the web build on main (#3608)
  • A voice saved or locked from a long take no longer repeats a word at the start of every chunk: takes keep their whole text, script markup such as [pause] never reaches a reference transcript, and voices saved earlier from a cut take transcribe their clip instead (#3062, #3602)
  • Radeon GPUs missing from a fixed remap table (RX 9070 on a gfx1200-only ROCm build, gfx1152, gfx1035) run on the GPU, and the setup GPU check names the installed PyTorch build and the ROCm opt-in instead of saying Windows has no ROCm build (#3418, #3602)
  • An audio.cpp runtime install on Windows no longer fails with "access denied" while antivirus scans the freshly checked binary (#2685, #3602)
  • The backend keeps answering while it frees an idle model's memory, instead of showing "not responding" (#3558, #3595)
  • First-run setup lets you continue once a different TTS engine is installed and selected, without the default model pack (#3581, #3595)
  • Dubbing's Cast picker always offers each speaker's Auto voice extracted from the video, so an override can be undone (#3582, #3595)
  • IndexTTS honours the Speed setting through its native duration control, and /ws/tts accepts a duration_factor (#3588, #3595)
  • An Intel Mac running from source now shows the remote-backend screen instead of an endless "Python environment is incomplete" error, and bun run setup:api explains why it cannot install (#3584, #3595)
  • The speech CLI keeps uploads well-formed for any file name, accepts numeric loopback addresses such as 127.1, and never sends local requests through a proxy (#3594) — thanks @rudycelekli!

  • Dubbed lines now start on detected speech, so a sigh, breath or footstep before a line no longer makes it start early, and trailing breaths no longer pad its slot (#3573, #3419)

  • First-run setup downloads large wheels such as the Windows CUDA torch over several connections and resumes them after Cancel or Retry, instead of crawling at about 1 MB/s on networks that limit each connection (#3557, #3411, #3060, #3202) — thanks @justcallme-A and @hadialshaer!

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v15.13.0 Stable2026-11-09T10:06:27Z

Critical fix

  • Cursor summaries: Cursor sessions got no summary on native Windows, or when the workspace path had a _, a space or a dot folder in it. claude-mem now looks for the transcript in the project folder Cursor actually names, so those sessions get summaries again (#5544, thanks @tanishqbaweja; the same symptom was first reported in #2248 by @wbingli).

New

  • Auto-clear at 33% context (off by default): turn it on with CLAUDE_MEM_AUTO_CLEAR_ENABLED=true. When a Claude Code conversation reaches a third of the context window, Claude saves its work state, claude-mem runs /clear and sends continue, and the fresh conversation picks the work back up. No save means no clear (#5486).
  • Standard Compute preset for the openai-compatible provider (#5492, thanks @henrikdukefoss).
  • PopNow preset for the openai-compatible provider (#5434, thanks @kyuminjo).

Fixes

  • Chroma: deleting many observations at once no longer piles up cleanups that lock chroma-mcp for hours; they now run one at a time (#5496, thanks @xunguangzlj-cloud; reported in #5495 by @voyvodka).
  • CLI: claude-mem --help, -h and a mistyped command print usage instead of starting a foreground worker (#5491, thanks @ryrenz; reported in #5490 by @ukdataservices-admin).
  • CLI: --flag=value works in server api-key, memory ingest and adopt, so --scope=memories:read really makes a read-only key (#5469, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Doctor: npx claude-mem doctor no longer crashes at exit on Windows (#5465, thanks @tng7576650-commits).
  • CMEM Pro sign-in: when cmem.ai refuses a sign-in start without a message, the installer shows the HTTP status instead of connectivity advice (#5498, thanks @xunguangzlj-cloud).
  • Redaction: vendor keys printed at the start of a line in tool output are now redacted (#5521).
  • Cowork: the Cowork shim redacts the NVIDIA, Runcrate, Groq, xAI and Perplexity keys the worker already redacts (#5483, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Windsurf: the installer writes hooks.json and the rules file atomically, so an interrupted install can't leave Windsurf with a broken hooks file (#5542, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Server: the viewer feed endpoints bound the offset a client can send (#5485, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Install: OpenCode, Kimi and Antigravity config files saved with a UTF-8 BOM are read correctly (#5472, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Launcher: a ~ at the start of CLAUDE_MEM_DATA_DIR is expanded when recording an empty hook payload, instead of creating a ~ folder in the project (#5473, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Import: re-importing an export keeps project merges (merged_into_project) (#5471, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • DSH: ingested tool memories keep the configured platformSource (#5470, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • CLAUDE.md: in a monorepo, folder CLAUDE.md rows are headed with a file that is in that folder (#5468, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • My Work: commits made with git commit -m "$(cat <<'EOF' ...)" and every gh pr title form are read correctly (#5480, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Search: file filters match whether paths use / or \ (#5466, thanks @liuyinchu).
  • Hermes: malformed endpoints and truncated responses fall back cleanly, sessions end even when the final event fails, and recall on long prompts stops at the query limit (#5462, thanks @Afloat16).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.12.1...v15.13.0

v15.12.1 Stable2026-11-08T22:09:47Z

Two critical fixes

  • macOS 12 (system SQLite 3.37): observations failed to save with "SQL logic error" on every write. Statements that use RETURNING are now read with .all(), so observations store again (#5499, thanks @xunguangzlj-cloud; reported in #5497 by @hamadikante0-ship-it).
  • CMEM Pro sign-in: since 15.11.0, sign-in stopped at its first step with "Could not start OAuth login". The installer now accepts the setup id that cmem.ai echoes into its sign-in links (#5502; reported in #5493 by @luciano-fiandesio).

Other fixes

  • Cursor: the installer writes hooks.json atomically, so an interrupted install or uninstall can no longer leave Cursor with a broken hooks file (#5484, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Worker security: the operator processing route rejects requests from foreign localhost origins (#5482, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Timeline: a #123 anchor is read as an observation id, not a date (#5479, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Prompt import: Claude Code transcripts and settings are read from CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR when it is set (#5476, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Server: truncating event payloads in the generation prompt keeps emoji and other surrogate pairs whole (#5478, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • Server: the legacy SERVER_BETA key is rotated when the canonical key is blank (#5477, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • OpenClaw: /claude-mem-search 4019 searches for the number instead of reading it as a result limit (#5474, thanks @rudycelekli).
  • OpenClaw: worker session posts are stamped with platform source openclaw (#5475, thanks @rudycelekli).

Also in this release

  • CI: superseded runs are cancelled, and the 5-OS image matrix runs only for image changes (#5481).
  • Tests: orphan-process snapshots skip unrelated system processes on Windows (#5501, thanks @liuyinchu).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.12.0...v15.12.1

longbridge/gpui-kit ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.1 Stable2026-11-09T08:51:00Z

GPUI Kit v0.9.1

GPUI Kit 0.9.1 is a backward-compatible patch release. It adds a draggable GridLayout, right-pinned table columns, Markdown task checkboxes, link tooltips and motion transitions that outlive their element, along with a large batch of input, text, slider, accessibility and gpui-shell hardening fixes. The default backend moves to gpui-pre 0.3.12 and the optional GPUI Fast backend to 0.1.14.

New APIs

  • GridLayout: a grid whose items the user drags and resizes, also registered in the gpui-shell component catalog. (#3770, #3789)
  • Table: ColumnFixed::Right pins columns to the right edge. (#3833)
  • Text: on_task_toggle makes Markdown task checkboxes clickable; block surfaces in TextView; pointer-following link tooltips. (#3811, #3830, #3793)
  • Motion: TransitionState for transitions that outlive their element. (#3841)
  • Components: Button::selected_accent, Slider::track_focus, Dialog::accessibility_label, TimeField::separator, Toolbar::axis for vertical toolbars, and a ratio-sized PieChart hole. (#3775, #3824, #3826, #3829, #3821, #3784)
  • WindowBorder: the frame colour comes from the window.border theme token, and window corners can be rounded on request. (#3849, #3848)

Editor and text input

  • Keep the first typed character in the editor search panel, compose decoration styles deterministically, draw a placeholder for a folded line, and show the arrow over the gutter. (#3772, #3777, #3791, #3806)
  • Tell the platform when the caret moves, publish layout at the end of prepaint, notify a moved input only while focused, and wait for the first layout before shaping an early value. (#3827, #3831, #3828, #3864)
  • Keep the caret's visual line at soft wraps. (#3842)

Markdown and text rendering

  • Supply editor foreground ranges through the render context and give span-less HTML paragraphs unique ids. (#3754, #3771)
  • Tables: highlight the hovered body row, select aligned cell text where it is drawn, and size columns to their images. (#3812, #3813, #3787)
  • Limit selection highlights to selected glyphs, draw unsized inline images at their own size, and avoid quadratic paragraph highlight merging. (#3799, #3786, #3846)

Components

  • Table / DataTable: name rows after their cells, centre default header names, Tab and Shift+Tab leave a focused DataTable, and a selected cell gets a border distinct from hover. (#3773, #3731, #3800, #3866)
  • Slider: allow min above the default max while building, snap dragged values to steps from min, and step one thumb from assistive technology. (#3823, #3822, #3840)
  • Menu: size items and default max width in rems, and open a scrollable menu on its checked item. (#3809, #3814)
  • Settings: follow locale changes in the search placeholder, clear search on Escape, show an empty state for unmatched searches. (#3815, #3816, #3692)
  • Motion: reverse an interrupted transition as CSS does, and keep value-transition timing under reduced motion. (#3820, #3839)
  • Scrolling: stop smooth-scrolling wheels at the edge on macOS, let the parent scroll a swipe over a fitting horizontal area, and keep the overscroll stream alive through sideways packets. (#3781, #3779, #3790)
  • List, Tab, WindowBorder: accent rows use accent_foreground, icon-only tabs keep their width, the element id path stays stable across decoration changes. (#3774, #3792, #3783)
  • Diff: reject invalid hunk prefixes before slicing UTF-8. (#3473)
  • WebView: fix an X11 abort when a WebView is dropped on Linux. (#3862)

Accessibility

  • Report a disabled SliderThumb to assistive technology. (#3825)

gpui-shell security and robustness

  • Require a network grant for WebViewState pages, accept only "*" as the unrestricted execute grant, and check Link.href against the URL scheme rule. (#3782, #3802, #3804)
  • Refuse bundle values that would break generated scripts, bound script-supplied counts, nesting depth and self-nesting views, and leave proxy URLs out of the default process.env. (#3803, #3805, #3808, #3817, #3810)
  • Stop a Git dependency selector being read as a git fetch option, honor the manifest entry when bundling, expose group hover to scripts, and shrink materialize_node frames. (#3778, #3682, #3796, #3807)

Dependencies

  • Update rustls-webpki and webpki-root-certs for CVE-2026-111943. (#3801)
  • gpui-pre 0.3.12 and gpui-fast 0.1.14. Under GPUI Fast 0.1.14, views that were not notified reuse the previous frame instead of rebuilding. (#3867)

FlashML-org/FreeToken ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.2 Stable2026-11-09T04:10:58Z

Highlights

  • Speculative decoding. --speculative-algorithm mtp drafts with the checkpoint's own MTP layer, and --speculative-algorithm dflash drafts with a separate DFlash 2 checkpoint (#836, #844, #856, #857).
  • New model. Ripple-65B Preview, from BF16 safetensors, the ModelOpt NVFP4 export or GGUF (#871).
  • Faster CPU experts in hybrid. The NVFP4 W4A8 CPU kernels are faster; rerun ft bench bw so hybrid uses the new CPU rate (#851).

Speculative Decoding

Each decode step drafts k tokens and verifies them on the target in one forward, so one step can commit several tokens. With sampling, the output distribution stays the target's. Speculative decoding is off by default, and the draft stays on the GPU, so it takes some KV and expert cache space. See the flags.

Target off MTP (faster of k=3, k=7) DFlash 2, k=7
Qwen3.8-27B (BF16) 50.1 144.4 (2.88x) 215.0 (4.29x)
Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (BF16) 193.1 359.6 (1.86x) 475.7 (2.46x)
Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-NVFP4 92.9 149.7 (1.61x) 174.2 (1.87x)

tok/s on an H100, one request, AIME25 problem 1, temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, median of 5 runs.

  • [Feature] --speculative-algorithm mtp: the Qwen3.5 family (Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, Qwen3.8-27B) and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, from HF or GGUF. A Flash-Next GGUF needs a Strata MTP pack (#836, #844, #856)
  • [Feature] --speculative-algorithm dflash: Qwen3.8-27B, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, with a DFlash 2 draft passed through --speculative-draft-model-path, such as incoai/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-DFlash2 (#857)
  • Not supported yet: TP above 1, and targets with sliding-window or DSV4 window-tier KV.

GGUF Support

  • [Feature] Serve gpt-oss GGUF checkpoints on gpt_oss (#838)
  • [Feature] Serve image input on qwen3vl GGUFs from a DeepStack qwen3vl_merger mmproj (#827)
  • [Feature] Serve image input on muse-glimmer GGUFs from a muse-glimmer mmproj (#830)
  • [Feature] Serve image input on minimax-m3 GGUFs from a minimax_m3 mmproj (#829)
  • [Feature] Add an IQ1_M MMQ kernel (#835)
  • [Feature] Read NVFP4 weight scales on qwen4exp GGUFs (#845)
  • [Perf] Sort MoE MMQ routes with a counting sort (#872, #878)
  • [Fix] Skip inactive GGUF routes in hybrid decode (#823)
  • [Fix] Log why mmproj auto-discovery skips a file (#860)
  • [Docs] Document GGUF support (#867)

Model Support

  • [Feature] Add Ripple-65B Preview support (#871)
  • [Fix] Read stop ids from config.json without generation_config.json (#874)

Engine

  • [Perf] Speed up the CPU MoE NVFP4 W4A8 kernels (#851)
  • [Perf] Give only the routes the CPU computes work items in a hybrid step (#866)
  • [Fix] Stop the SWA prefill commit from freeing pages the request still reads (#651)
  • [Fix] Send graph-capture KV writes to the dummy page (#877)
  • [Fix] Add once-per-message logger methods (#858)
  • [Docs] Add a guide to speeding up decode (#859)

Kernels

  • [Fix] Avoid overflow in Triton KV cache offsets (#854)
  • [Fix] Size the decode KV tile to the shared-memory budget (#840)

Server and CLI

  • [Feature] Report per-request prefill and decode timings in API responses with --enable-timings-report (#814)
  • [Feature] Add continuous_usage_stats and count shell tok/s from it (#868)
  • [Fix] Recover Qwen tool calls after unclosed think (#825)
  • [Fix] Refuse n != 1 on /v1/completions instead of ignoring it (#842)
  • [Fix] Reject empty completions prompt and keep error code (#850)
  • [Fix] Bound top-k before int32 conversion (#790)

AMD ROCm (WIP, experimental)

  • [Fix] Publish ROCm CPU expert inputs before notification (#764)

Breaking Changes & Deprecations

  • qwen3vl / qwen3vlmoe, muse-glimmer and minimax-m3 GGUF models with a fitting *mmproj*.gguf in their folder now build the vision tower. --text-model-only keeps them text-only (#827, #829, #830).
  • /v1/completions with n != 1 now returns 400 instead of one choice (#842).

Contributors

@jason-fxz, @ischencheng, @void-mckenzie, @LZ-QWQ, @madbrain76, @festeraeb, @A5bhinav, @zihaomu, @wenlive, @techsaurabh-394

  • @madbrain76 in #825
  • @festeraeb in #840
  • @A5bhinav in #842
  • @wenlive in #790
  • @techsaurabh-394 in #850

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v18.18.3 Stable2026-11-09T03:59:10Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Changed

  • Added a balanced delegation bias between eager and restrained; models without a delegation-bias rule now resolve to it instead of eager

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • The ask tool takes one HTML form ({ html }) instead of questions[]: the model writes a <form> of radios, checkboxes, selects, text inputs and textareas with its recommendation preselected, and receives the submitted form data as JSON. AskToolDetails is now { answers: { name, question, answer }[]; timedOut? }; QuestionResult, the "Other", "Chat about this", answer-note and answer-image paths of the tool are removed, and /tree re-answer reopens the original form (NavigateTreeResult.reopenAsk carries html).

Added

  • In Tern, right-clicking an ask answer offers Note, which annotates that answer without picking it (#18551 by @H4vC)
  • task.eager (preferred/always) now shapes only the main agent's system prompt; subagents fall back to their model's own delegation policy instead of being pushed to spawn further subagents
  • ask forms can show pictures: <img src> from an http(s) URL, a data: URI or a local file (relative to the session's working directory), and inline <svg> diagrams, drawn in the terminal form and natively in Tern
  • ask forms give every radio and checkbox group a free-text "Other" answer: its text is the radio's value or is appended to the checkbox group's array (field-by-field fallback, collab guests and ACP elicitation included)
  • Extension UI gains optional ui.formDialog(html, options), which renders an HTML <form> and resolves to { data, timedOut? } (undefined when cancelled). The interactive TUI renders it as a form dialog, ACP clients with form elicitation get one elicitation with a property per field, and hosts without it (RPC) answer the ask tool field by field through select/input/editor.
  • Eval cells can run shell commands with sh: sh("git status") in Python, await sh`ls ${dir}` or await sh("make", { cwd }) in JavaScript. Commands run on the embedded bash engine to completion (never auto-backgrounded), return their output, and raise ShellError with the exit code on failure.
  • omp zsh on Windows runs omp's builtin utilities (ls, cat, head, jq, …) for commands PATH does not have, unless a command_not_found_handler handles them (#18532 by @H4vC).

Changed

  • Clarified tool and internal-URL descriptions for history:// concise transcripts, proc:// job retention and parked-agent lookups, and read JSON query text output (#18553 by @xiangnan0811)
  • Models without a delegation rule (Claude, Gemini, …) no longer get pushed to delegate: the "map unknown code via task" rule and the "Task + scout" nudges in search tool descriptions are replaced by a light suggestion to explore unfamiliar subsystems with a scout
  • In Tern, clicking an ask answer and then Note now annotates the answer highlighted on the question shown instead of the one just clicked (#18551 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed ask notes added with n right after picking an answer landing on the previous question instead of the one shown; a Tern click then Note still notes the answer just clicked, and the Note button names it (#18317 by @DonMikone)
  • Fixed /export failing on Windows when saving in the current directory, including relative paths such as sub/../session.html; the export message now shows the full path of the written file (#18369, #18525 by @minhngoc25a).
  • Todo initialization now names empty phases Tasks, so subsequent updates can address them (#18552 by @xiangnan0811)
  • omp zsh and the bash tool on Windows: background commands without job control ignore Ctrl-C (INT, and QUIT in zsh) as zsh and bash do, so the console's Ctrl-C leaves them running (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh finds the system zsh's function library (compinit loads) on Debian, Ubuntu and Arch, and on Windows from an MSYS2, Cygwin or Git for Windows zsh (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh on Windows: typed commands no longer end in ^M, programs are found through Path, piped scripts run every line, cd takes C:\…/C:/…, .. and ~ (keeping $PWD as C:/…), >/dev/null works, the prompt is % rather than root's #, $OSTYPE is msys, and no USERNAME warning is printed (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh on Windows edits lines with ZLE (history, Tab completion, key bindings, $COLUMNS); Ctrl-C abandons the line, runs an INT trap, or interrupts a read that then goes on, instead of ending the shell (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh on Windows: Ctrl-C interrupts the foreground program, jobs, fg and bg work (a job brought back with fg gets Ctrl-C too), and process substitution reaches Windows programs (diff <(…) <(…), prog >(…)) (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • On Windows, /dev/null works in the bash tool (>/dev/null, cat /dev/null, [ -c /dev/null ]) (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • On Windows, /dev/tty opens the console in omp zsh and the bash tool (prog >/dev/tty), and an MSYS or Cygwin program killed by Ctrl-C reports status 130 instead of 512 (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh prints the [n] pid line for a background pipeline under job control, as it does for a single command (#18532 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh starts compinit many times faster: single array and association elements are assigned and read without copying the whole parameter, and autoload -r no longer re-reads the .zwc digests in $fpath for every function it resolves (#18533 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh's read -k and read -q read a key from the terminal without waiting for Enter, as zsh does, instead of reading standard input (#18533 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh completion listings mark file types again (dir/, exe*), print -c lays words out in columns without a terminal, and ${x?msg} ends a non-interactive shell with status 1 as zsh does (#18533 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh floats keep full precision in arithmetic (s=$EPOCHREALTIME; print $(( EPOCHREALTIME - s )) no longer drifts by up to a second) and still print in their -F/-E style (#18533 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh's typeset -U path / typeset -U fpath (and local -U path) drop repeated entries as zsh does (#18533 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh and the bash tool: $! of a background pipeline is its last program's pid, as in bash and zsh, so kill $! signals that program; omp zsh's [n] line for a background pipeline lists the pid of each of its processes, as zsh does (#18547 by @H4vC).
  • omp zsh is faster: function calls, loops and arithmetic run 2–3× faster, compinit almost 4× faster, and building a large array or association one element at a time (a+=(x), h[k]=v) or reading $a[i] from a large array no longer slows down with its size (#18565 by @H4vC).

@oh-my-pi/collab-web

Changed

  • ask tool cards show the HTML form's fieldset legends and field count while pending, and each question with its submitted answer (or "(no answer)") once answered, flagging submissions made by the timeout

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Breaking Changes

  • The ask tool renderer now draws { html } form calls and AskToolDetails { answers, timedOut } results; QuestionResult and the question-list detail fields were removed.

Added

  • Added a Note entry to the right-click menu of each ask dialog answer row in Tern, annotating that answer without picking it (#18551 by @H4vC)
  • Added HtmlFormDialog, which renders an HTML form as an interactive terminal form, splits long forms into pages, and returns the answers as JSON. Single <select>s are one-row dropdowns, radio and checkbox groups get a free-text "Other" answer, <img> (URL, data: URI or local file) and inline <svg> pictures load in the background, an optional inactivity timeout submits the current values, and Tern terminals get the form natively.
  • Added a cursor: "start" option to Editor.setText so prefilled multi-line text opens at its first line.

Changed

  • The ask dialog's Note right after a pointer click now annotates the highlighted answer of the question shown instead of the answer just clicked, and the Note button no longer names a redirect target (#18551 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed the ask dialog's n right after a keyboard pick annotating the previous question's answer instead of the highlighted row shown; only a pointer click still redirects Note to the answer just picked, and the Note button then names it (#18317 by @DonMikone)
