View release →

v18.18.2 Stable2026-11-08T18:07:14Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • Added canReplayRemoteCompactionFrom() to determine whether a model can replay provider-native compaction produced by another model.

Fixed

  • Fixed GenAI message-content capture configured in omp's own .env files so it continues to apply when those values are no longer exposed through the process environment.
  • Fixed compaction preparation for remote OpenAI compaction that the active model cannot replay, ensuring the content is expanded and remains available for compaction.
  • Fixed generic remote compaction requests to /chat/completions so gateway sessions retain the correct per-request model attribution.

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added edit execution context support for Cursor handlers, allowing integrations to identify frames that materialize native file edits and detect when server-side edits have settled.
  • Exported Responses replay utilities so callers can determine which turns are sent as native payload items when estimating or trimming context.

Fixed

  • Fixed rotated credential pools reporting a stale sibling credential's authentication error instead of the exhausted credential's usage-limit status and reset time.
  • Fixed OpenAI image edits with multiple input images failing because image parameters were duplicated.
  • Fixed Cursor-provider reads of xd:// devices failing due to incorrect device URL handling.
  • Fixed Anthropic sessions getting stuck after refused turns containing thinking or redacted-thinking blocks when followed by a steer, reminder, or new prompt.
  • Fixed Codex Responses requests losing tool namespaces and developer-provided additional tools when replayed through the authentication gateway.
  • Fixed Gemini malformed responses being treated as unhandled stop reasons and losing token usage; they are now handled as transient transport errors.
  • Improved Cursor compatibility with TLS-intercepting proxies that block HTTP/2 by falling back to HTTP/1 for subsequent turns.

@oh-my-pi/browser-relay

Added

  • The browser extension now requests Chrome’s downloads permission so relay tabs can report where downloads were saved to omp.

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Fixed

  • Fixed model catalog pricing and output-limit metadata for Mistral alias IDs, including the Mistral Vibe CLI default model, by applying the metadata of their aliased models.
  • Updated xAI Grok 4.20 beta and multi-agent aliases to use the current Grok 4.20-0309 pricing, including the higher rates for prompts exceeding 200K tokens.

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • logoutCredential() now accepts an AgentSession instead of a model registry and session ID. Hosts must install a scoped-model refresher with AgentSession.setScopedModelsRefresher(); InteractiveMode and InteractiveModeContext no longer accept refreshScopedModelsAfterLogin.
  • SessionStorage.hasAssistantTurn() is replaced by the optional scanLines(path, visit), which session listings use to recover both a missed assistant record and a first prompt past the 4 KB head; custom storages that implemented hasAssistantTurn() must implement scanLines() instead.

Added

  • The shell's ls builtin supports macOS/BSD -O: a long listing gains the file-flags column (hidden, compressed, uchg, restricted, …) instead of rejecting the option, so ls -lO works and flags are visible again.
  • The shell ships sd as a builtin, so sd FIND REPLACE [FILES]… find-and-replace (regex or -F literal, in place or -p preview) works without the binary installed.
  • Added omp gc --strip (or gc.strip), which removes provider replay data (thinking signatures, encrypted reasoning, cached native responses, request controls) from sessions idle for gc.stripAfterDays (default 30, --strip-after-days), including subagent and archived transcripts; stripped sessions still resume on any model.

Changed

  • The sloppy edit tool (*** Find/*** Replace) now rejects only the operations that fail to match and applies the rest, including across files; the result lists each rejected operation against the file as written, and the call fails only when no file changes. Payloads that do not parse still apply nothing.
  • A bash call that overlaps another one (parallel calls, or a call while one runs in the background) now runs in a copy of the session shell, so it sees the session's variables, functions, options, and working directory from before the other command; what it sets still does not persist.
  • The ./c continue shortcut now interrupts a running turn and continues it, in the main session and in focused subagent views, instead of failing with "Agent is already processing" or doing nothing.
  • Updated system prompts with the ⁂ section marker and improved task completion guidance, including proportional verification, efficient file reading, batching independent operations, and stopping after changes are verified.
  • Added faster isolated-subagent startup for ovnfs-backed checkouts, while preserving the existing baseline behavior for repositories with nested repositories or submodules.
  • Improved extension and plugin management: /extensions now lists and toggles extensions from configured extension packages and installed plugins, and marketplace sources resolve relative and bare-name paths consistently with Claude Code.
  • Scoped model lists now refresh after logout, model-catalog refreshes, and RPC login or logout, so available models stay synchronized with authentication and provider changes.
  • Added source actions to LSP code_actions, including actions such as Organize Imports, with application by title or index.
  • Browser automation now supports downloads from relay tabs and broader role/ selectors for implicit landmarks and structural roles.
  • Improved skill and session workflows: skills created or installed during a session become immediately discoverable, and user-attributed custom messages that start a turn receive the appropriate prompt setup.
  • Validated security findings now display validation status, timestamp, and summary information.
  • Dual-mode language servers retain pushed diagnostics alongside pull diagnostics, preserving build and lint errors across edits.
  • Improved session navigation and reliability when switches, forks, branches, context resets, or new sessions overlap; requests are processed in order without freezing or misrouting messages.
  • Improved eval and interactive execution behavior, including preservation of JavaScript state and output across asynchronous failures, clearer startup and runtime errors, and better reporting when kernels or runtimes terminate.
  • Parent-to-subagent messages now appear as IRC cards in live and Agent Hub transcripts.
  • Paused agent turns can allow the machine to sleep at safe boundaries and restore sleep prevention when resumed.
  • Improved JSON-mode shutdown handling so final tool events and buffered output are preserved.
  • Improved task efficiency and responsiveness for isolated subagents, file search, browser guidance on Linux/X11, and long-running or cancellable operations.
  • Updated the tui.mouse setting documentation to accurately describe wheel-scrolling behavior.
  • mnemopi.dbPath values beginning with ~ now resolve relative to the home directory.
  • Disabling the Codex provider now prevents its configuration from overriding omp settings, and providers can be disabled without applying their other settings.
  • omp gc now shows a progress bar for each slow step (listing and scanning sessions, scanning transcripts for blob refs, archiving) on interactive terminals.
  • omp gc --strip and --compress start work immediately instead of first reading every session's metadata, and --strip processes several sessions at once with a running tally of transcripts stripped.
  • omp gc without --apply now says up front that it is a dry run.

Fixed

  • Fixed omp --resume showing (no messages) for sessions whose first prompt follows a large memory recall, image-only message, or /skill: reply (#16495)
  • In the shell visualization, a running command's inline script, heredoc, or sh -c/ssh script stays undimmed with the command instead of fading as inactive.
  • The shell visualization highlights heredocs and inline scripts behind launchers (uv run python -, timeout 60 python3 -c, env … bun -e, ssh host 'bash -s' <<EOF, ssh host <<EOF, docker run … bash -euc), names a heredoc by its #! line or the file it writes (cat > "$dir/lib.rs"), and highlights Rust, Go, C-family and Swift heredocs.
  • On Windows, commands run by the bash tool, including nohup background commands, get a console of their own, so programs that draw directly on the console (xperf and PowerShell progress bars, password prompts) no longer corrupt the TUI (#18517 by @H4vC).
  • Reading Reddit URLs now works when Reddit blocks its API: posts, comment threads, subreddits, and user pages fall back to a Redlib mirror and then the Arctic Shift archive, and render full nested comment trees.
  • Limited xAI web_search to two hosted-search turns per request to help prevent unexpected X search costs.
  • Fixed extension event delivery so each extension receives events in emission order, even when another extension is slow to process an earlier event.
  • pi.sendUserMessage() now rejects when a message is dropped before a turn begins, including the cost-cap reason when available.
  • Prevented OpenTelemetry settings from omp-managed .env files from leaking into child processes or in-process extensions.
  • Supervised-service waits now complete only after logs, metadata, and session-owner state have been published.
  • Fixed Windows daemon metadata loss when another process temporarily reads meta.json.
  • Improved image-generation errors to report why each model in a failed fallback chain was unsuccessful.
  • Fixed model switching after remote compaction so the destination provider can recover the necessary conversation history instead of receiving only a placeholder summary.
  • Remote MCP HTTP and SSE connections now honor NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS from the agent environment, including certificates loaded after startup.
  • Preserved provider prompt-cache reuse across auto-learn captures, skill updates, and other session changes that do not require rebuilding the full system prompt.
  • Closing /btw history during tool approval now correctly returns focus to the approval prompt.
  • URL reads now handle bot-protection challenges more reliably, try alternate user agents, and report blocked or failed results instead of returning a protection page as content.
  • Timed-out or cancelled JSON reads now stop their jq processing promptly.
  • Fixed standalone binaries loading extensions that import top-level @oh-my-pi/pi-utils subpaths.
  • HUD job status updates now continue to reflect elapsed time instead of freezing until matching output arrives.
  • Generic remote compaction now supports before_provider_request payload hooks.
  • Background completions no longer start hidden turns after an ACP prompt ends or incorrectly leave the session busy.
  • Fixed shell behavior for exit status and EXIT traps in subshells to match Bash semantics.
  • Preserved provider prompt-cache hits when resuming sessions after pruning, rewinding, branching, tree navigation, or other history changes.
  • Cursor native edit operations now display accurate edit cards and diffs, including failed edits, instead of misleading separate read/write cards.
  • grep and glob now respect timeout and cancellation when ovnfs status or daemon handshakes stall.
  • Fixed --mode json shutdowns so tool completion events and the final JSON record are not lost or truncated.
  • Commands beginning with cd / now execute from the filesystem root as intended.
  • Edit auto-repair now accounts for retry costs and stops before exceeding the session cost cap.
  • MCP prompt commands now correctly assign positional arguments that follow key=value arguments.
  • /shake thinking now reports and preserves reasoning blocks that the provider continues to replay, avoiding inaccurate context-reduction claims.

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed recall returning episodic summaries for working memories that had been invalidated.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • Added Zig syntax highlighting for fenced Zig code in Markdown.
  • Added Shell.fork(), a new shell whose session starts from this one's variables, functions, aliases, options, and working directory as of its last command boundary, without waiting for a command in flight.

Fixed

  • Built-in jq filters now stop reliably when aborted or timed out, even before producing output.
  • Fixed logical directory changes with cd and pwd in native Windows shell sessions, including drive and UNC paths.
  • Fixed closed file descriptors passed to external commands on Unix so writes fail as expected; closed standard streams on Windows now use the null device.
  • Fixed setValue() on Linux reporting success when Firefox or Thunderbird fields, including password fields, were not actually updated; failures now identify the affected field appropriately.

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Fixed

  • Fixed the trace dashboard returning HTTP 431 for sessions with many subagents.

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • Added a model catalog refresh callback that lets applications update the active model scope after catalogs are re-fetched.
  • Preserved unfinished answers in ask dialogs and custom editors, allowing users to resume drafts when reopening them.

Changed

  • Simplified Bash previews for redirection to /dev/null by showing a single icon in the Nerd Font symbol preset.

Fixed

  • Fixed streaming and scrolling display issues in terminals without synchronized output, including duplicated rows and blank screens or status lines.
  • Fixed Markdown rendering while streaming list items, preserving bold text and heading formatting.
  • Fixed wrapping of long embedded Bash arguments so indentation and the gutter remain aligned.
  • Fixed prompts and Enter presses entered while the application was starting so they submit normally instead of leaving Enter as a newline.
  • Fixed restored transcripts so aliased tool calls use the same rendering as live tool calls.
  • Fixed debugger tool cards with tabs and control characters in arguments so their displayed width remains accurate.
  • Fixed LaTeX styling in nested content, including bold and italic resets, radical and optional-argument styling, color scoping, and inline math in headings.
  • Fixed unnecessary repainting in the setup wizard while waiting for input in ANSI terminals.
  • Fixed Windows ConPTY event handling so delayed terminal event data no longer appears in the composer.
  • Fixed OSC 8 hyperlink support in Rex terminals so transcript URLs and file paths are command-clickable.

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added utilities for reading OpenTelemetry configuration from omp-managed .env files, including $otelEnv and withOmpOtelEnv.
  • Added filesystem path utilities for checking paths through symlinked roots, including paths that do not yet exist, and for optionally propagating filesystem errors in directory checks.

Fixed

  • Fixed relaxed JSON parsing so __proto__ is preserved as a regular own property rather than modifying the parsed object's prototype.
  • Fixed SIGHUP handling so stopped terminal sessions display recovery hints, including the --resume command.

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.58.1.

View release →

studio-v2.58.1Reasonix Studio v2.58.1 Stable2026-11-09T01:07:25Z

本版是修复版：修好 2.58.0 里设置页版面错位、会话行置顶按钮常驻、模型菜单标签折行、思考「展开」被限高等问题，修复 Windows 启动闪控制台窗口、命令行 mcp --help 误执行和 Linux 终端选择。

变更

  • 桌面壳的 Electron 升到 45.2.3（Chromium 156，含上游的安全与稳定性修复）。关联 #15027。

修复

  • 修复 Linux 上「在终端中打开」不管你把哪个终端设成默认，总是按固定顺序挑一个（比如装了 konsole 就先用它，而不是你的 kitty）。现在如果装了 xdg-terminal-exec（桌面规范里读取默认终端的启动器），就由它按你机器声明的默认终端打开；没装的话仍按原来的顺序（#15031）。
  • 修复命令行里 reasonix mcp import --help（以及 mcp remove --help 等其他子命令）不会显示用法，反而把 --help 当成参数真的去执行：import 会直接改写 MCP 配置。现在所有 reasonix mcp 子命令的 --help / -h 都只打印用法并退出，什么都不改；mcp import 带多余参数时会报错而不是照常导入（#15022）。
  • 修复 2.58.0 里，开着「会话行快捷按钮」时，侧栏每一行会话都一直显示一个置顶图标（不管有没有置顶）：现在这个按钮只在鼠标移上去或键盘聚焦时才出现；已置顶的会话在没悬停时会一直显示一个小图钉，方便一眼看出（#15012）。
  • 修复输入框右下角的模型选单里，模型名字较长时，右边的「读图」标签被挤成两行竖着排的问题；现在标签始终在一行里完整显示（#15012）。
  • 修复 2.58.0 里设置的「模型」「连接」等单栏页面：顶部「本页包含」目录和下面的标题、卡片没有对齐，卡片左边多出一大块空白（现在三者对齐到同一条左边线）（#15008）。
  • 修复从「操作与性能」点开「导航入口」这类二级页时，顶部多出几条空白的横条；现在二级页里只显示它自己（#15009）。
  • 修复设置里「思考过程」选成「展开」后，模型思考时仍然只显示最新六行、要等整段思考结束才一下子全部显示的问题：选「展开」时，运行中的思考块现在随内容一直完整展开；「进行中展开」仍是最新六行的滚动窗口（#15013）。
  • 修复 Windows 上每次启动 Reasonix Studio 都会先闪一下黑色控制台窗口的问题：启动时用来确认"是不是同一个 Studio"的内核探测，以及启动内核本身，现在都隐藏控制台窗口（#15016）。

<!-- lang:en -->

Changed

  • The desktop shell moves to Electron 45.2.3 (Chromium 156, with upstream security and stability fixes). Refs #15027.

Fixed

  • On Linux, "Open in terminal" picked a terminal by a fixed order rather than the one the machine declares as its default (konsole before kitty, for example). When xdg-terminal-exec is installed, it now starts the machine's default terminal; without it the old order still applies (#15031).
  • reasonix mcp import --help (and --help on the other mcp subcommands) did not print usage and ran the command with --help as an argument, so import rewrote the MCP configuration. Every reasonix mcp subcommand now prints usage for --help / -h and changes nothing, and mcp import refuses extra arguments instead of importing (#15022).
  • With "Quick buttons on session rows" turned on, every session row in the sidebar showed a pin icon all the time, pinned or not (2.58.0). The button now appears only when the pointer is over the row or it has keyboard focus, and a pinned session keeps a small pin on the row while nothing is hovered (#15012).
  • In the model menu next to the message box, the "reads images" tag was squeezed into two stacked characters beside a long model name; it now stays whole on one line (#15012).
  • In Settings, single-column pages such as Model and Connection no longer draw the "On this page" index out of line with the title and cards, which left a wide empty strip beside the cards in 2.58.0; all three now share one left edge (#15008).
  • Opening a sub-page such as Navigation entries from Operation and performance no longer leaves a few empty bars above it; the sub-page shows only itself (#15009).
  • With Thinking set to "Open", a thought still showed only its latest six lines while the model was thinking and then jumped to the full text when it finished. In "Open" the running thought now grows with its text; "Open while running" keeps the six-line rolling window (#15013).
  • On Windows, launching Reasonix Studio no longer flashes a console window first: the kernel probe that tells whether this is the same Studio, and the kernel launch itself, now run with their console hidden (#15016).

What's Changed

  • chore(deps-dev): bump electron from 45.1.0 to 45.2.3 in /desktop/electron in the npm group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15027
  • chore(deps): bump the npm group in /desktop/frontend-next with 11 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15029
  • chore(deps): bump the actions group with 4 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15028
  • chore(deps): bump the go group with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15025

Full Changelog: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/compare/studio-v2.58.0...studio-v2.58.1

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.65 Stable2026-11-09T00:19:32Z

A message an agent sends to a session on another device now says who sent it. It used to arrive there as plain text, indistinguishable from that session's own user. It now carries the same sender line and authority notice as a message between sessions on one device (the sending session, its workspace and project, and the device it came from), and a leading / in it no longer runs a command or expands a prompt template on the receiving side. omo remote send from a terminal is unchanged. (#12858, part of #12852)

Memory retention no longer evaluates its completion-record guard twice per record. The completion sweep now reads the guard once and keeps or removes the record on that single answer, and the retention tests check their own preconditions instead of asserting them away. No change to what retention keeps or removes. (#12783, follow-up to #12532)

The standalone omo binary's omo doctor reports memory runtime size again, like the npm install. Each memory identity's runtime size against its cap and the total across identities are printed by both distributions, so binary/npm doctor parity holds (#12785). (#12783)

Old binary runtimes are pruned safely when two omo launches start at once. A launch no longer deletes a runtime another launch is still pruning, and a runtime that launch already removed is not reported as a failure. omo --version, --help and omo update never prune. (#12770, follow-up to #12627)

A task child whose engine cannot hold its designated model chain is no longer started. Before, when a process child's engine could not take its chain (an older engine, a refused set_retry_fallback, or no answer in time) or a task host did not advertise retry_fallback_profile, the child was still prompted and ran on the engine's own fallback settings, which can reach models outside its chain. The start now fails before the first prompt and names every designated model and the reason, and such a host is refused as incompatible, naming the same models. (#12759) (#12761)

A command on a closed session on another device now reports the same error as on this device. It answered session_not_found remotely and unknown_session locally; both now answer unknown_session. (#12755) (#12756)

omo hands its device keys to the device service from memory, with no credential store read for relay. A fresh sign-in (omo login, omo account adopt) deletes the signed-out marker and then hands its in-memory keys and account to a running device service. The memory sync session re-adopts from the credential it already holds when the service reports no adopted device. Every sign-out path (logout, forget, a session the service ended) tells the service to forget the device. The start-up bootstrap no longer reads the stored credential. device.adopt carries account only to a device service at 0.1.3 or newer, and the pinned device service is raised to 0.1.3 (design D PR 4). (#12735)

omo remote can open, send, switch model, interrupt and close sessions on your other devices. omo remote open <device> [--project P] [--model M] starts a session there and prints <device>/<session>; omo remote send <device>/<session> <text> prints the answer as it streams in and returns when the turn settles (--steer steers the running turn, --follow-up queues behind it, --no-wait returns once the session took the message); model, interrupt and close do what they say. Each verb is the same remote op the agent's thread tools use, and --json prints its typed result. A steer to a session on another device now reaches it when that device's engine reports its running turn, instead of always being refused. (#12709, part of #12320)

Creating or forking a thread on another device no longer waits for a separate session.locate round trip: the create asks the relay for the new session's id on the open_session reply and uses it. A relay daemon without that answer (an older host, or an older service on this device) is handled as before, with session.locate. (#12686)

omo update on the standalone binary now replaces the installed omo. It used to print a replace command for the copy under ~/.omo/binary-runtime/<version>/, which the next launch put back, so the update never took. A launch also removes old runtime versions there, keeping the previous one for a rollback and any version still running. (#12627, fixes #12626)

Each memory identity's runtime directory is capped at 2 GiB, and omo doctor and /doctor show how much it holds. After retention, the 12-hour memory maintenance pass evicts oldest-first until the identity fits memory.runtime.max_bytes (default 2 GiB, 0 for unbounded, per-agent overridable under memory.agents.<id>.runtime). It removes folded reflection runs first, then the transcripts of fully reflected sessions, idle sidecars, and finally old failed runs beyond the newest ten. Journals that were never or only partly reflected are kept by default; memory.runtime.discard_unreflected_journals: true lets the cap evict them too, and only while reflection is succeeding on the identity. Live sessions, in-flight runs and the run-id high water are never touched. omo doctor prints each identity's runtime size and the total, and warns when an identity reaches 80 % of its cap. /doctor adds a runtime-size check with the largest stores and what retention would still free. (#12538)

Memory no longer grows without bound on disk. The background memory maintenance pass now removes finished reflection runs, fully reflected transcripts, recall sidecars and legacy stores past configurable windows (memory.runtime.retention_days 7, completions_days 30), in time-boxed slices that resume where they stopped. Transcript input reflection never folded into memory is kept unless you turn on discard_unreflected_journals (then removed after abandoned_journal_days, 14, only while reflection works for that agent). Nothing a live session owns, no locked or unfinished work and no run id reflection still needs is touched. (#12532)

A reflection lock left behind by a run whose processes died (launcher killed before the run started, supervisor and child gone with no outcome, an unreadable run ledger, or a torn active.lock) is now released at session start, on the next turn, and by the new omo doctor --fix (--dry-run to preview, /doctor --fix in a session), instead of keeping every later reflection queued; omo doctor --fix also reclaims runtime locks whose owner is gone. (#12517)

Fixed

  • Senpi: omo now names any senpi feature it relies on that the running senpi lacks in one startup warning, and opening a monitor on another device's session on a senpi without remote wake fails with remote_wake_unavailable instead of starting a watch that never wakes. A contract test checks every such feature against the pinned senpi. (#12862, fixes #12861)
  • The browser skill now depends on the published omowright package (^0.3.0, the first release that ships a built dist/ that loads under Node as well as Bun) instead of a git commit. Its runtime stager and loader accept omowright's TypeScript sources, the older JavaScript sources, or a compiled dist/, in that order, so a future layout change cannot silently break the staged browser runtime (#12842, #12851, #12847). (#12847)
  • Memory facts extraction records facts again with senpi 2026.11.6-3: the facts child (also used by onboarding's understand step) keeps the approval mode its own settings give it and may run only its own record tools without asking, instead of being refused as a child whose parent is gone. (#12839)
  • Concurrent memory account resolves on a desktop host share one open, so the app sees one descriptor request instead of one per caller; a failed open is retried by the next resolve (carried from #12717, which this PR supersedes). (#12839)
  • Thread tools: an agent can now reach a session on another device of the same account by its name. A name that matches the title of one live session on another device sends there, and a name shared across devices (or with a thread on this device) is refused as ambiguous with every candidate and its device listed, never picked silently. Claiming or renaming a thread alias on a signed-in CLI device now reaches the account's alias registry instead of always answering that the registry cannot be reached; an alias claimed on one device now reaches that thread from every other device instead of reporting its device offline, and a registry outage never changes how a plain name resolves. omo thread read resolves aliases and other devices' threads the way the agent's thread_read does. (#12837, fixes #12833)
  • Release: the service-auth release step now refuses a bundle that carries more than one release key set, so it always checks the set the bundle ships. (#12812, fixes #12617)
  • Runtime fallback: when a session times out and no fallback model can be prepared, the user is now warned even if nothing was awaiting a fallback, the warning names the real reason ("no available fallback models" while attempts remain, instead of "max fallback attempts reached"), and a compaction that outlives its watchdog budget returns control as a real cancellation without staying marked as recovering. (#12790, fixes #12762)
  • Standalone binary on Windows: running the provisioned runtime copy directly (~/.omo/binary-runtime/<version>/omo.exe) no longer hands off to itself and reports itself as the installed launcher when the home path is spelled with an 8.3 short name or different letter case. (#12772, fixes #12769)
  • While service accounts are closed, the compiled omo binary recognises the device-code omo login with the same option parse as the command itself, so omo login -- --device and similar argv get the immediate refusal instead of being handed on to the command. (#12723, fixes #12646)
  • The browser skill's doctor no longer calls a browser ready when its only BrowserSkill extension is 0.4.0. That release left a "BrowserSkill" tab group in the user's browser after every task. The doctor now reports outdated-extension and tells the user to update the extension and delete the saved groups. (#12663)
  • omo no longer warns "Could not download the OmO device service. Check the connection and try again." on every interactive start while the device service release it pins has not been published. A 404 for that release is now its own not_published state: the start stays quiet, and omo remote bootstrap says remote devices are not available in this build yet. A connection failure, a server error or a half-published release still warns. (#12659)
  • When a dream or reflection run deletes, archives or moves a memory note, [[path]] and relative markdown links to it in other notes are now updated in the same run, after the completion check has decided which of the run's changes land. A moved note's links follow it, and the moved note's own relative links keep pointing at their targets. A note the run archived, meaning its ARCHIVE.md entry names the file (its path, or its file name when no other note shares it), is linked as [[ARCHIVE.md|<old path> (archived)]]. Links to any other removed note, such as one the user asked to forget, become plain text. A note the completion check refuses, or one whose rewrite it would refuse (for example a note that already holds secret-like text), or one that is not valid UTF-8, keeps its links as they were, so /doctor can still report those. A targeted /dream --to <doc> that removes its own target is no longer refused because of these link updates. Facts extraction also no longer writes into a month ledger path that was archived or deleted: that month's new facts go to notes/facts/<month>-2.md (then -3, ...), so an old link can never start pointing at unrelated new content. (#12642) (#12645)
  • Runtime fallback keeps compaction retries separate from the primary model chain and preserves substantive retry prompts. Compaction watchdogs defer within a bounded budget, exhausted native retry signals do not trigger an abort, and stuck-session watchdogs still return control when no replacement can be dispatched. Thanks @mradwankhalil. (#11907, fixes #9786)
  • Background tasks let runtime fallback decide who recovers a session error before starting a replacement or finalizing the task. Exhausted and refused retries release recovery ownership, model-less retry errors can finish the task, and a slow decision never starts a competing replacement. (#9683, #11907)
  • Runtime fallback keeps its place in the fallback chain when its own abort echoes back as a stop or idle event, and no longer mistakes its own retry for a manual model change. A failing fallback now moves on to the next model instead of going back to the model that just failed. (#11901, fixes #6751)
  • Runtime fallback: a session timeout whose fallback dispatch is rejected now uses up the attempt, stops at max_fallback_attempts, and ends with an abort the caller sees instead of re-arming the timer forever. (#11898, fixes #6637)
  • First-prompt refusal tests now await launch settlement when the bounded start report arrives first, preserving both untyped and non-credential refusal diagnostics without depending on subprocess startup speed (#11732). (#11852)

Changed

  • Task children ask through the parent session instead of being refused: a child's permission prompt (and its other select, confirm, input and editor dialogs) is raised on the parent's UI, so Allow runs the child's call and Deny does not. A child the parent turn is waiting on asks on the connection that started that turn; a background child asks every attached client that may answer. Secret inputs and permanent grant selections are never forwarded. Children in their own process or on the task host open in the parent's approval mode and follow every later change of it, one change at a time and always ending on the latest; a child that could not follow is reported to the parent session. (#12839)

  • omo remote open and the thread tools' create and fork on another device name the approval mode the new session runs in: the creating session's own mode, or for omo remote open the new --permission-preset flag, else this device's own setting; a setting that cannot be read is a usage error rather than the loosest mode. (#12839)

  • The compiled engine no longer has the unused installEngineCaTrust startup routine. That routine set NODE_USE_SYSTEM_CA=1 but had no caller, and engine launches set up certificate trust in the launcher trust stage. A new test checks that an engine started without NODE_USE_SYSTEM_CA in its environment does not get it from the launcher. Validation of OMO_EXTRA_CA_CERTIFICATES is unchanged. (#12628, part of #10762)

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.9.26 Stable2026-11-09T00:05:05Z

HyperFrames v0.9.26

Released on 2026-11-08.

Cloud renders now find media whose file names have accents, even when macOS stores the name differently from the page. A large project's first history copy is also much faster.

Fixes

  • CLI: Cloud render finds media whose file name is stored in another Unicode form than the page's (91ad7fb6e, #7419).

Performance

  • Studio Server: A large project's first history copy takes a fraction of the time (2927fe615, #7415).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.25...v0.9.26

View release →

v0.9.25 Stable2026-11-08T20:25:21Z

HyperFrames v0.9.25

Released on 2026-11-08.

Re-rendering a film with the same settings now gives the same result, because every render seeds its random values. A muted video's picture also no longer waits on its own audio track at a cut, and check replays an unchanged project's last pass.

Features

  • CLI: Check replays an unchanged project's last pass and takes --no-craft (070b964d7, #7398).

Fixes

  • Core: A muted video's picture no longer waits on its own audio track at a cut (fa2e63515, #7412).
  • Producer: Seed random values in every render so re-renders give the same film (eb16510a1, #7410).
  • Core: Capture pages load only the clips on screen (12906e0bf, #7402).

Internal

  • Install ffmpeg through the GitHub API asset route with a cache (074de9dd1, #7401).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.23...v0.9.25

phuryn/pm-skills ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.3.0 Stable2026-11-08T23:10:06Z

v2.3.0 — 2026-11-08

pm-execution

  • Added live-qa: audience Q&A for an all-hands, AMA, customer webinar, launch, sprint review or talk. Anonymous questions, upvotes deciding the order, moderation before anything reaches the screen, questions opened before the session, and every unanswered question leaving with an owner and a date. It ends with a FAQ sent where the audience is and a short report. AskOne is the default tool (disclosed: it is made by this collection's author); Slido, Mentimeter and Pigeonhole Live work with the same method, and the skill says where the Q&A built into meeting tools falls short (in Zoom, upvotes need every question visible, so questions can't be screened first). With AskOne's MCP server connected, the agent creates the room, moderates, marks questions answered and drafts the FAQ.
  • Added live-polls: polls, dot-voting, confidence votes, word clouds and quizzes in meetings, workshops and training. Votes are collected before the discussion with results hidden until the vote closes, the decision each result drives is stated before the vote, and the result is recorded beside the decision. AskOne by default, with Mentimeter, Slido, Poll Everywhere, Kahoot and the meeting tools' own polls named; with its MCP server the agent prepares the polls as drafts and launches each one when its topic comes up.
  • retro, pre-mortem, prioritization-frameworks and stakeholder-map now say when to collect input anonymously and before the discussion, and point to the two new skills.

pm-market-research

  • Added survey-design, built on The Ultimate Guide to Effective Customer Surveys: whether you need a survey, a live poll or interviews; the decision and research questions before any question; who answers and the coverage gap; a pilot; twelve rules for writing questions; response-rate levers; non-response and low-quality answers; and CES, CSAT, NPS and Jobs-to-be-Done templates.

pm-go-to-market

  • gtm-strategy names a launch webinar or AMA with live audience Q&A among launch-day activities.

Everything else

  • pm-ai-shipping moved to phuryn/product-engineering, which maintains the AI Shipping Kit in its ai-shipping plugin. Its three skills and five commands keep their names here and only say where each one went and how to install it, so an agent that loads one can tell you. Once product-engineering's plugin is installed, turn this one off.
  • Added Cursor plugin manifests: a root .cursor-plugin/marketplace.json and a .cursor-plugin/plugin.json in every plugin, mirroring the Claude manifests. Claude manifests are unchanged.

New in this release

Target support

  • Radeon GPUs - @gusteivos

Improvements

  • NVIDIA:
    • NVLink support - @danbedford
    • ECC support for professional GPUs - @LH-and-FPGA
    • Extra clock domains not reported by NVML (XBAR, SYS, NVD) - @Panchovix

    • PCIe RX/TX load in the utilization charts - @Basavaraja-MS

  • NVIDIA:
    • Correct MIG mode handling for process utilization - @Henry-ZHR
    • UMA memory fallback and unified-memory accounting - @redyuan43, @sudoingX, @johnnynunez
    • Do not report a fake PCIe link on unified-memory SoC GPUs - @Code4me2
    • Show 0% instead of N/A when there are no new utilization samples - @wuzhonglijz

    • Fix startup segfault and NVIDIA detection under WSL2 - @zzccppp

    • Count GTT in per-process memory on integrated GPUs - @rborkow
    • Fall back to hwmon junction temperature on SMU13.0.6 - @jason34105533
    • Fix divide-by-zero when the power limit is missing - @JoseOliveira-beep
  • Intel:
    • Fix ~20 MB/hour heap growth from per-refresh udev devices - @bqv
    • Read the xe GT frequency from sysfs instead of a perf event - @stolk
    • Fixes for Intel Xe cards and memory frequency reporting - @Steve-Tech
  • fdinfo:
    • Apply the graphical default per process, not per fd - @stolk
    • Cap the engine busy percentage instead of dropping samples, avoiding a division by zero - @ViderStar
  • Interface and snapshot:
    • Make --no-processes work with --snapshot and --loop - @yinghao-w
    • Fix null cmdline in snapshot output - @gitfool
    • Fix window cleanup leaks - @danbedford
    • Respect the hide-processes option from the config file - @yinghao-w
    • Increase the elapsed-time buffer on the timeline - @aadamowski
    • Clamp GPU and memory clock percentages to 100 - @bqv
    • Fix the shader core count field - @Panchovix
    • Guard clock-rate plots against a zero maximum, drop stale processes of unmonitored GPUs
  • Other:
    • MSM temperature and current clock reporting - @fromdisposition
    • Various Ascend, Qualcomm and V3D fixes

Misc

  • Dockerfile fix for Docker builds on Windows 11 - @piro4you
  • Documentation for invalid TERM/ncurses errors - @raravind007
  • Snap: terminfo packages for more terminals - @simonjgreen

A huge thanks to all the contributors and testers :tada:

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.28CC Switch v4.0.28 Stable2026-11-08T15:56:25Z

CC Switch v4.0.28

本版本主要应对 Codex 11 月 8 日的不兼容更新。

⚠️ Codex 桌面版 26.1103、CLI 0.167 起，官方订阅和第三方供应商的会话彻底分开了。 直连模式下在两者之间切换，以前只是两边的会话分开显示，现在另一边的会话直接变成只读：能查看，不能继续，继续时提示「模型提供商不匹配」。要来回切换并继续旧会话，有两个办法：

  1. 改用路由或聚合模式：本版本起，路由或聚合模式下切到第三方，会话和官方订阅留在同一组，来回切换都能继续。
  2. 打开「统一 Codex 会话历史」（「设置 → 应用配置 → Codex」）：官方和第三方的会话归到同一组，直连模式下也能来回切换。

此外，直连切换让会话变成只读时会提示数量；开启「统一 Codex 会话历史」并勾选迁入现有会话后，会持续把官方会话迁进来。官方额度用完、Codex 锁住发送键时，可以在路由或聚合设置里打开「暂停使用官方登录」，继续用第三方模型；官方额度保护改为默认开启。应用页的安装和升级会显示排队状态和实时进度。另外修复了 Codex 故障转移到别家时仍带着上一家的模型名、Kimi For Coding 上压缩失败、在 Kimi 或 DeepSeek 上续接工具调用后会话一直报 400 等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

适配新版 Codex 的会话分组

Codex 按供应商标识记录会话：官方订阅记在内置的 openai 下，CC Switch 写入的第三方供应商记在 custom 下。新版 Codex 只能继续与当前供应商同一组的会话，另一组只读（#7974）。本版本围绕这一点做了以下改动：

  • 路由或聚合模式下切到第三方，会话不再分成两组：本机登录了 ChatGPT、且没有开「统一 Codex 会话历史」时，路由到第三方也和官方一样写成内置的 openai，请求照常经本地路由发往第三方，所有会话都在 openai 这一组。第三方的 Key 由本地路由替换，ChatGPT 的账号 ID、组织等请求头会被去掉，第三方收不到你的账号信息。
    • 以下情况仍写成 custom：本机没有 ChatGPT 登录、开着统一会话历史、打开了「暂停使用官方登录」，或者供应商配置里写了请求重试、流重试、流空闲超时、远程压缩这些内置 openai 表达不了的设置。
    • 已经在用路由模式的不用操作，CC Switch 下次接上路由时会自动改写一次。直连模式不变。
  • 直连切换后提示有多少会话只能查看：没开统一会话历史时，直连模式下在官方订阅和第三方之间切换，切换成功的提示里会多一行，例如「之前在官方订阅下的 12 个会话现在只能查看」。从托盘切换不提示。
  • 统一 Codex 会话历史（「设置 → 应用配置 → Codex」）：开启后官方订阅也改用 custom 标识，两边的会话在同一组，新版 Codex 里都能继续。经常在官方和第三方之间切换时建议开启。
    • 「同时迁入现有官方会话历史」改为默认勾选。勾选后，之后每次启动 CC Switch 都会把又出现在官方组的会话迁进来，比如关掉再打开过这个开关，或者不经 CC Switch 用过 Codex。
    • 迁移和还原前都要备份 Codex 的会话数据库，以前 42 MB 的数据库要备份约 90 秒，现在不到 1 秒。
    • 后台迁移完成后会提示迁入了多少个会话，重启 Codex 后就能继续。

暂停使用官方登录（Codex）

官方 ChatGPT 额度用完后，Codex 会自己查额度，锁住模型选择和发送键，选了第三方模型也发不出去（#7974、#7962）。这一步在 Codex 客户端里，本地路由管不到；以前手动把 requires_openai_auth 改成 false 的办法，现在切换一次就会被覆盖。

现在 Codex 的「路由设置」和「聚合设置」（路由或聚合模式生效时，模式标签栏右端的齿轮按钮）最上面多了「暂停使用官方登录」开关：

  • 打开后 Codex 不再使用官方登录，不会因为官方额度锁住发送键，聚合和路由里的第三方模型可以继续用。官方登录仍保存在本机，不会退出。
  • 打开期间，官方模型发不出去，会提示改用第三方模型；Codex 里不显示账号和用量信息，第一次打开可能弹出新手问卷；侧栏「项目」里原来的会话暂时看不到，关掉开关后会回来；新会话记在 custom 组，切回官方后只能查看。
  • 打开期间 Codex 页面顶部会一直有提示，官方额度恢复后点「恢复官方登录」即可。
  • 打开和关闭后都要彻底退出并重新打开 Codex。只在路由和聚合模式下生效，直连不受影响。

应用页显示安装进度

应用页的安装、升级和卸载一次只跑一个，以前慢的时候每一行都在转圈，分不清是在下载、在排队还是卡住了（MiniMax Code 的安装包约 100 MB，网络慢时要十几分钟）。现在排队的那一行会写「排队中，等前一个应用完成」，正在跑的那一行显示已用时间和命令输出的最新一行。离开应用页再回来，进度还在。

变更

  • 官方额度保护改为默认开启：经本地路由的 Codex 官方请求，在五小时使用率达到 95% 或每周使用率达到 98% 时就会暂停，第三方模型照常可用，避免走到 Codex 锁住发送键那一步。升级后会自动打开一次；之后手动关掉，不会再被打开。阈值在「设置 → 应用配置 → Codex → 官方额度保护」里改。
  • 不再写入 disable_response_storage（#7524）：新版 Codex 删掉了这个设置，每次启动都会提示有一个无法识别的配置项。预设、模板、统一供应商和一键导入链接都不再写它，已有 config.toml 里的这一行在下次切换时清掉。
  • Antigravity 显示 Argon 每周额度（#8913）：Pro 套餐的 Argon 每周额度以前不显示。现在卡片上把它并进 Gemini 那一行，下拉菜单和授权中心单独列出。
  • Windows 上第一次启动更快：新建数据库时建表和写入内置模型价格各合成一次提交，第一次启动快了约 6 秒。

修复

  • Codex 故障转移到别家时，仍带着上一家的模型名，备用供应商报模型不存在（#7547）。现在开着故障转移时，换到备用供应商前会先看它认不认请求里的模型（是它自己的模型，或在它的模型列表里），不认就换成它自己的模型；同一个模型的中转队列照原样发。图片生成、图片编辑和搜索请求不换模型。
  • Codex 经路由连 Kimi For Coding 时，压缩上下文总是失败（HTTP 400）。Kimi 不接受压缩请求里的 tool_choice: "none"；现在第三方原生 Responses 上游拒绝压缩请求时，会去掉 tool_choice 再试一次。
  • Codex 在 Kimi 或 DeepSeek 的原生 Responses 上续接工具调用后，这个会话之后每一轮都报 400。工具调用和它的结果之间夹了一条助手消息，Kimi 和 DeepSeek 都会拒绝整段历史；Kimi 还会拒绝空的助手消息。现在只对 Kimi（Moonshot）和 DeepSeek 的地址整理这类历史，其他请求原样发出。
  • Windows 上 Codex 目录在 WSL 里（\\wsl.localhost）时，分叉出来的会话用量一直不导入。

致谢

感谢 @allen546（Antigravity Argon 每周额度）、@fisHarly0（SSE 分隔符说明）的贡献。

mksglu/context-mode ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.1.7 Stable2026-11-08T15:00:33Z

Fixes

  • fix: #1338 source test follows the D1 helper (d1UpdateCommand)
  • fix: doctor D1 in an unsupported host shows the host line, not the Gemini update command (#1340)
  • fix: the #1338 ctx_upgrade test follows the platform: no CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR words on Windows by design
  • fix: CLI doctor names an unsupported host only when no supported agent is found; the background-hold note says the process was stopped (review of 2.1.6..c8180a97)
  • fix: doctor D1 names the CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR profile like ctx_upgrade; Pi and OpenCode are never marked unsupported by a terminal env var (review of 2.1.6..7bb515e8)
  • fix: doctor D1 update is WARN with the agent's own command, D3 reads every OpenCode config and tuple entries, D6 PASS for in-process plugins (#1339)
  • fix: setup renews a stale Claude Code plugin copy with the host's own update and ends in a WARN instead of a rollback; custom CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR profiles are named in doctor and ctx_upgrade (#1338)
  • fix: unsupported-host lines live in one module; Pi and Pi-Bolt never unsupported (test)
  • fix: doctor and setup say clearly when a host is not supported
  • fix: ctx_execute settles on exit plus a bounded pipe drain; killTree never blocks the host loop (#1337)
  • fix: policy.json routes.webfetch off and allowHosts turn off the WebFetch route (#1336)
  • fix: doctor D3 names Antigravity once instead of wrong Gemini entries (#1340)

Who gets this

One version on both channels: the release commit on main (GitHub) and npm.
